# Rain Slime
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Rain Slime]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 6
Spawn Location: [[Spider's Den]]
Mob Type: Cubic ([[Cubic (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 200; [[Damage]]: 50
[Drops]
Drops: 3–5x [[Slimeball]]
XP: +9 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 6
[Metadata]
ID: RANDOM_SLIME_8
The Rain Slime is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Spider's Den]].
## Spawning
Rain Slimes spawn naturally in the [[Spider's Den]] when it is raining, at the base of the mound the [[Grandma's House]] is on, and between [[Michael's Crib]] and the mound [[Flint Skeleton|Flint Skeletons]] spawn on.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv6]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Slimeball]]|3-5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv6]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|2|2|2|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|9|9|9|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|6|6|6|
Rain Slime can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Rain Slime Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+1,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Spider's Den ([[Bestiary/List]])|Rain Slime|These Slimes enjoy rain...and jumping to their deaths.|4|15|1,000|

## Strategy
Since Rain Slimes do not multiply upon death, players will not have to worry about that when dealing with them. Some of the larger slimes have a lot larger reach than they seem to, so if the player does not have much [[Effective Health]] and can get one-shot by them, the player should probably fight them from afar with a bow. Also, because these slimes deal heavy knockback, even if the player is not killed directly from the mob, the fall damage can take out many early-game players.

A Rat is a [[Pests|Garden Pest]]. Its corresponding crop is [[Pumpkin]]. It is attracted to [[Tasty Cheese]].
## Spawning
Rats can spawn in the [[The Garden]], starting at [[The Garden|Garden V (5)]].
## Behavior
Rats run away from the player. Rats can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

# Rat (Hub)
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, The Uprising
[Rat]
Type: Zombie
Mob Level: 3
Spawn Location: [[Sewer]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5000
[Drops]
Drops: 3–5x [[Tasty Cheese]] 0–1x [[Rat Pet]]
XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 3
[Metadata]
ID: RAT_1
Rats are mobs that spawn in the [[Sewer]].
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv3]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|3|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Tasty Cheese]]|2|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|50%|
|[[Rat Pet]]|1|6Rare Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.2%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv3]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|3|3|3|
A Rat can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or [[Hunting|Hunted]] with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to obtain a [[Rat Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]]. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Behavior
Rats have 5,000 [[Health]] and move at a speed of 3 blocks per second.
Rats respawn every 3 [[Time Systems|SkyBlock days]] (60 minutes).
## Trivia
- This [[Mob]] was added as part of 50[[Ranks|[MINISTER]]] 56ms' (Swavy) Minister perk due to his position as a [[Resistance General]].
  - The perk itself is a reference to 50[[Ranks|[MINISTER]]] 56ms' fans calling him a rat, which originated from him titling streams "Rat Hunting" while playing the game Escape From Tarkov.
- It is possible to capture a Rat soul by using an item that has the Raise Souls ability. This is the only soul with 0 [[Damage]] and also the only one that costs 0 [[Mana]] to summon. The soul will appear as a [[Zombie]] after it is summoned by a player.
  - Since Rats count as zombies, killing one with a Zombie Sword gives you the It Never Ends achievement.
- There was a common bug that made a rat's damage box sink into ground, making it almost invulnerable. Some workarounds were using a [[Shadow Fury]], [[Explosive Bow]], or [[Phantom Rod]].
- Killing a Rat actually counts towards the 10 Zombie kills for the [[Quests|Time to Strike]] Questline.

# Redstone Pigman
Aliases: Redstone Pigmen
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Redstone Pigman]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 8
Spawn Location: [[Pigmen's Den]]
Mob Type: Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])
Stats: [[Health]]: 250; [[Damage]]: 75
[Drops]
Drops: 0–5x [[Redstone Dust]] 0–2x [[Gold Nugget]] 0–1x [[Flaming Sword]] 0–1x [[Exp Share Core]]
XP: +12 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 8
[Metadata]
ID: REDSTONE_PIGMAN_10
A Redstone Pigman is a mob found in the [[Pigmen's Den]].
## Behavior
Redstone Pigmen are neutral as long as players don't attack them or have less than 64 (1 stack) [[Redstone Dust]] in their inventory. If one player attacks the Pigman or has more than one stack of Redstone Dust in their inventory around a Redstone Pigman, they will become aggressive to all players around. This effect will notify you when you get a message in chat informing you that the pigmen are angry at you for taking their redstone, as well as displaying fire particles around them. Provoking a single Redstone Pigman will also provoke all others around, causing them to attack the aggressor. They deal 75 [[Damage]] on hit.

## Trivia
- Although Redstone Pigmen hold a [[Pigman Sword]], their ability to shoot out Burning Souls was removed in an unknown update.
- Redstone Pigmen have the appearance of a Zombified Piglin, however, they use the sounds of Piglin.
- Redstone Pigmen are the only mob able to drop [[Runes|Redstone Rune]], which is the only rune they can drop.

## Behavior
The Revenant Horror is a Zombie-type mob which is hostile towards all nearby players. It moves at a relatively fast pace and deals damage with its scythe weapon. It receives a speed boost on hit.
All Revenant Horrors are immune to [[Potions|potion effects]]. They are immune to [[Bows|arrows]]; when hit by them, they heal. They are immune to the item abilities of [[Aspect of the Dragons]] and [[Ice Spray Wand]].
As with all Slayer bosses, if the Revenant Horror has not dealt damage to a player for some time, it will begin rapidly regenerating [[Health]]. The regeneration immediately stops when it hits a player again. Additionally, if either it or its target stays in the same block for too long, it performs a slam-dunk attack, knocking the target towards it and dealing [[True Damage]]. If 3 minutes pass without the boss being defeated, it will despawn, failing the Slayer quest. A timer is shown above its head indicating the remaining time.
If the Revenant Horror enters the Village area, it will immediately despawn and the Slayer quest will be failed.
Tier I through Tier IV Revenant Horror have all the abilities of the tiers below them.
|Tier|[[Health]]|[[Damage]]|Ability|Slayer XP Reward|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|I|500|15|Life Drain Drains [[Health]] every few seconds|5 XP|
|II|20k|25|Pestilence Deals AOE damage every second, shredding armor by 25%|25 XP|
|III|400k|120|Enrage Gets real mad once in a while|100 XP|
|IV|1.5M|400||500 XP|
|V|10M|2,400|Redemption Heals rapidly and is immune to magic damage.|1500 XP|
|V|10M|2,400|Explosive Assault Throws explosive TNT.|1500 XP|
|V|10M|2,400|Thermonuclear Charges up and releases a massive explosion.|1500 XP|
### Tier III and Tier IV
After 40 seconds, it will enter a "Mad" state, in which its behavior is not changed. After another 5 seconds, it will enter an "Enraged" state, in which it moves slightly faster and deals highly increased amounts of damage. This enraged state lasts for 12 seconds.
### Tier V (Atoned Horror)
The Tier V boss is much different from the bosses preceding it. It has a different appearance and is named "Atoned Horror" instead of "Revenant Horror". It is also immune to all types of ability damage, [[Wither Skeleton Pet]]'s Death's Touch ability, as well as damage from [[Bonemerang]]. It has a natural regeneration effect, which heals for 1/1000 of its maximum health(10,000 [[Health]] normally, 20,000 [[Health]] with [[Derpy|Mayor Derpy]]) per second.
Every second, a circle will appear beneath its target's feet similar to the [[Gaia Construct]], but made of different blocks and larger in size. The center is red and progresses to white. After a short delay, the Atoned Horror will throw a block of TNT to the center of that circle. The TNT explodes on contact with the ground, damaging enemies within the circle for a percentage of their health, depending on how close they are to the circle's center. When the player is hit by the TNT the boss heals 500,000 health. Upon reaching 1/3 health, the boss enters an enhanced stage, throwing TNT twice as often. The boss remains in this state even if it regenerates health back to above 1/3.
After 30 seconds, it will stop moving and attacking, and a circle of expanding bedrock forms around its feet. A text appears above its head labeled "Boom!" and a hidden timer counts down from 5 seconds. Nearby mobs flee from the Atoned Horror when this happens. Upon reaching 0, the Atoned Horror unleashes a barrage of lightning around it, similar to [[Storm]]'s Giga Lightning, killing anything within a 7 block radius. This attack bypasses protection from the [[End Stone Sword]] and [[Mithril Coat]]. After that, it resumes attacking as before.
## Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.
|Mob|Stats|Spawned at/via|Mob Types|Item Drops|Non-Item Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv114] [[Revenant Sycophant]]|[[Health]]: 50,000; [[Damage]]: 400|Tier III [[Zombie Slayer]]|Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|1x [[Revenant Flesh]]|+855 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 342 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv179] [[Revenant Champion]]|[[Health]]: 250,000; [[Damage]]: 800|Tier IV [[Zombie Slayer]]|Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|2x [[Revenant Flesh]]|+1,342 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 537 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv217] [[Deformed Revenant]]|[[Health]]: 500,000; [[Damage]]: 1,200|Tier IV [[Zombie Slayer]]|Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|5x [[Revenant Flesh]]|+1,627 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 651 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv271] [[Atoned Champion]]|[[Health]]: 1,000,000; [[Damage]]: 2,400|Tier V [[Zombie Slayer]]|Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|2x [[Revenant Flesh]]|+1,626 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 813 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv329] [[Atoned Revenant]]|[[Health]]: 2,500,000; [[Damage]]: 3,600|Tier V [[Zombie Slayer]]|Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|5x [[Revenant Flesh]]|+1,974 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 987 [[Experience]]|
## Tips
- Any of the [[Dragon Armor|Dragon Armors]] reforged to [[Reforging|Fierce]] are recommended options if the player is looking to take down a Revenant Horror Tier III.
- For the Revenant Horror Tier IV, using [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] reforged on to [[Reforging|Ancient]] or a better armor set, and paired with a max enchanted [[Reaper Falchion]] can help immensely.
- It is possible to kill the Tier IV or lower very quickly using a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Guardian Pet]] with a highly intelligent Armor Set such as Necrotic [[Wise Dragon Armor]] or [[Storm's Armor]].
- If the Revenant Horror gets enraged, it is advised to run away until it reverts to its normal state.
- If the player or the boss stays on the same block space for too long, it will result in the player taking [[True Damage]] and massive [[Knockback]] from the boss. This only applies to Tier III and above, however.
- Trapping the Revenant Horror in a lift in the [[Deep Caverns]] can make the fight a lot easier. Additionally, spawning the boss in the [[Diamond Reserve]] or [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] and luring it into [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] can hugely reduce the Revenant Horror's mobility and prevent it from landing hits on the player; however, it can still use Life Drain which can drain the player's [[Health]] from a distance.
- For Tier III or higher bosses, healing items such as Power Orbs and weapons with [[Life Steal]] or [[Drain]] are essential.
- When standing at [[Coordinates|-138, 42, -141]] in the [[Crypts]], the Tier V [[TNT]] attack cannot hit the player. Therefore, using this spot can let players focus on [[Damage]] instead of high [[Effective Health]].
## Trivia
- The Atoned Horror was leaked by former admin 5[[Ranks|[MVP+]]] Minikloon. It was shown in a stream of StudioTV. The boss had 3M [[Health]] and the TNT was significantly weaker.
- Because it is immune to arrows, the Revenant Horror is also immune to the effects of [[Voodoo Doll]] and will not be affected by the damage over time debuff.
- The Revenant Horror, the Tarantula Broodfather and the Riftstalker Bloodfiend are currently the only Slayer bosses to have a Tier V variant.
- Under the leadership of [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] and [[Resistance General|FurryEBoy]], a single Tier V Revenant Horror provided enough [[Combat]] XP to immediately spawn another Tier V Revenant Horror. Players would pair up to spawn Tier V Revenant Horrors for each other.
  - This was possible with [[Derpy]]. It was possible to slayer trade with Svens & Tarantulas as well. Furthermore, with the addition of the [[Attributes|Veteran]] attribute after the Kuudra Revamp, this was possible even without the XP bonus from Mayors. However, this was patched in an update, which a mob gives at max 75% of the [[Combat|Combat XP]] required.
- When using the [[Sentinel Warden Skin]], the boss stops changing its appearance when enraged.

# Revenant Sycophant

The Revenant Sycophant is [[Mob]] and [[Revenant Horror]] / [[Zombie Slayer]] [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]] that has a chance to be spawned when a player with an active [[Revenant Horror]] Tier III or higher Quest kills a [[Zombie]]-type Mob. When this happens, an explosion effect takes place and the Revenant Sycophant spawns.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv114]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Revenant Flesh]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|

|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|855|855|855|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|342|342|342|
Revenant Sycophant can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Sycophant Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+1,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Behavior
It runs somewhat quickly, dealing 320 [[Damage]] upon contact.

A half-vampire, half-thrall, immortal golem creation representing the multiverse coven in combat.
— Maddox the Slayer
The Riftstalker Bloodfiend is the [[Slayer]] [[Bosses|Boss]] in the [[Vampire Slayer]] branch. It is unique to the [[Rift Dimension]] at the [[Stillgore Château]], and requires [[Sven Packmaster|Sven Packmaster III]] completion to access.

## Behavior
When the Riftstalker Bloodfiend is spawned, all nearby Vampires except the Bloodfiend himself and the Clotgoyles spawned by him are passive against the player who spawn the boss.
|Tier|[[Health]]|[[Damage]] (in [[Hearts]])|Ability|Slayer XP Reward|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|I|625 [[Health]]|0.5|Coagulation Deals 0.5/1/1/1/1.5[[Hearts]] of damage every second to whoever spawned the boss.|10 XP|
|I|625 [[Health]]|0.5|Spectral Form Once in a while or when too far from a player, transforms into a bat and teleports around.|10 XP|
|I|625 [[Health]]|0.5|Mania The boss becomes immune for 26s.; During that time, the boss spawns a circle of blocks split into 4 rings.; Stand in the safe ring at the right time or take 2/3/5/6/15[[Hearts]] of damage.; Being outside of the 13 blocks circle incurs 3/3/3/6/6 damage every second.|10 XP|
|II|1100 [[Health]]|1|Twinclaws Every 7s, launch two giant claws. One in front, one behind.; The frontal claw deals 3/5/6/8 [[Hearts]] of damage.|25 XP|
|II|1100 [[Health]]|1|Stonewrath #1 Clotting Spawns 3 Clotgoyles, which each strip a [[Hearts]] from you while alive. Killing them turns them into cadavers.|25 XP|
|II|1100 [[Health]]|1|Stonewrath #2 Stonewrath The boss flies up and starts aiming at your location. After 2s it will lock in, letting you move out of the target before it explodes. The explosion will destroy any cadaver for good.|25 XP|
|II|1100 [[Health]]|1|Stonewrath #3 Resurrect A few seconds after the explosion, the boss respawns any surviving cadaver.|25 XP|
|III|1800 [[Health]]|1|Killer Spring The boss launches a spring made of candles to a nearby location.; Compact it by clicking on it while alternating left and right click.; The spring must be fully compacted within 15s or everything explodes and you die.|60 XP|
|III|1800 [[Health]]|1|Impel Complete the boss's order within 2.5/2/2s or take 4/5/20 [[Hearts]] .|60 XP|
|IV|2400 [[Health]]|1||120 XP|
|V|3000 [[Health]]|1.5|Blood Ichor Frequently spawns an ichor nearby which must be destroyed within 25s.; Destroy it by having the boss launch Twin Claws at it.|150 XP|
### Tier I
The Riftstalker Bloodfiend constantly drains 0.5 [[Hearts]] every second. Every few seconds[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.), or if the player is too far away, it will enter its Spectral Form to move. It will return back to normal once it lands. Once the boss is at half health, it will use its Mania ability, becoming immune for 26 seconds. A circle of red blocks will spawn, along with a green ring inside of it. The player must stand in the green ring to not get their 2 [[Hearts]] taken. Being outside of the 13 blocks circle incurs 3 [[Hearts]] every second. As a vampire, when at or below 125 [[Health]], it takes +1000 [[Rift Damage]] when attacked by a player with a [[Steak Stake]].
### Tier II
Instead of draining 0.5 [[Hearts]] every second and taking away 2 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania, the boss now drains 1 [[Hearts]] every second and takes away 3 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania. Now, the Mania phase starts upon the boss reaching 60% health. Every 7 seconds, a pair of Twinclaws spawn, with one in front of the boss and one behind the boss. The frontal claw is shot towards one of the players fighting the boss, and takes away 3 [[Hearts]] upon hitting any players fighting the boss. The boss also spawns Clotgoyles that reduce the player's maximum [[Hearts]] by 1 after their Mania ability. After the Clotgoyles are killed, the hearts are returned to the player. They will respawn unless hit by the Riftstalker Bloodfiend's Stonewrath. As a vampire, when at or below 220 [[Health]], it takes +1000 [[Rift Damage]] when attacked by a player with a [[Steak Stake]].
### Tier III
Instead of taking away 3 [[Hearts]] per Twinclaws attack and 3 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania, the boss now takes away 5 [[Hearts]] per Twinclaws attack and 5 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania. The boss has 2 Mania attacks now, starting at 75% [[Health]] and 40% [[Health]]. During Mania attacks, the boss uses its Impel ability, during which players have to complete the boss's order (one of the following: CLICK UP, CLICK DOWN, JUMP, SNEAK) within 2.5s, or else 5 [[Hearts]] are taken away. After the first Mania attack, the boss starts the Stonewrath ability. After the second Mania attack, the boss launches a spring made of candles to a nearby location. It can be compacted by clicking on it while alternating left and right click. The spring must be fully compacted within 15s or everything explodes and all players fighting it die. As a vampire, when at or below 360 [[Health]], it takes +1000 [[Rift Damage]] when attacked by a player with a [[Steak Stake]].
### Tier IV
Instead of taking away 5 [[Hearts]] per Twinclaws attack, 5 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania and 3 [[Hearts]] when out of the Mania circle, the boss now takes away 6 [[Hearts]] hearts for each of these attacks. Additionally, impels now have to be completed within 2s. The Twinclaws attack can now be used during the Mania attacks. As a vampire, when at or below 480 [[Health]], it takes +1000 [[Rift Damage]] when attacked by a player with a [[Steak Stake]].
### Tier V
Instead of taking away 6 [[Hearts]] per Twinclaws attack, 6 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania, 5 [[Hearts]] per failed Impel and draining 1 [[Hearts]] per second, the boss now takes away 8 [[Hearts]] per Twinclaws attack, 15 [[Hearts]] per failed Mania, 20 [[Hearts]] per failed Impel and drains 1.5 [[Hearts]] per second. The boss has a new Blood Ichor attack, which frequently spawns an ichor nearby which must be destroyed within 25s. When Twinclaws are thrown towards the Blood Ichor, it breaks. As a vampire, when at or below 600 [[Health]], it takes +1000 [[Rift Damage]] when attacked by a player with a [[Steak Stake]].
## Tips
- Due to [[Unfanged Vampire Part]] being relatively common, and gear for this slayer not being particularly expensive or difficult to obtain, regardless of player's progress outside of the Rift Dimension, fighting Tier IV or Tier V of Riftstalker Bloodfiend can be quite a good way to grind [[Coins]].
## Bugs
- The sounds from the Killer Spring ability on Tier 3 and above may overload Minecraft's sound engine, resulting in all game sounds being completely muted for a few seconds.
  - This can be alleviated by muting the entity.wither.spawn sound using a mod (such as Sound Controller) or a resource pack. The mod SkyHanni also provides an option to mute excess sounds without muting wither sounds entirely.
- During Stonewrath, the Tier V Riftstalker Bloodfiend is able to spawn Blood Ichors, resulting in Blood Ichors spawning in inaccesible spots.
  - This can be partially mitigated by making sure to spawn the boss somewhere the boss won't be able to spawn an ichor during Stonewrath.
  - Or phase the boss into Mania before it casts Stonewrath to completely avoid this bug
## Trivia
- Although the ability description for Mania says it lasts 22s, it actually lasts 26s.
- Damage from Coagulation happens every 1.5 seconds. This causes each tick to deal 0.75/1.5/1.5/1.5/2.25 [[Hearts]] , and for Tier V with an active Blood Ichor, it becomes 3 [[Hearts]] per tick.
- The [[Motes]] earned from spawning and killing the Riftstalker Bloodfiend can pay off the cost of starting the Slayer Quest.
  - As such, this slayer has potential to guarantee profit both inside the Rift Dimension and out. The [[Vampiric Melon|Vampiric Melons]] and [[Splatter Crux|Splatter Cruxes]] dropped by spawning mobs can be sold for [[Motes]] in addition to most boss drops. The rest can be sold outside the Rift Dimension for [[Coins]].
- The ability Impel is derived from modern vampire media in which vampires are sometimes depicted as beings capable of hypnotizing their victims with a single glance.
  - Hypnosis is not found in traditional vampire folklore, but instead first appeared in works of 19th-century writers such as Bram Stoker, who popularized the idea of vampires using mesmerizing gazes to entrance their victims. In modern vampire media vampires are occasionally shown to mind control their victims, forcing them to do their bidding.
- Despite the [[Combat]] skill or its leveling rewards not having any impact in the Rift, the Tier V Riftstalker Bloodfiend requires [[Combat|Combat XXV (25)]] to summon.

Scary Jerry is a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]] that spawns in [[Wilderness]] and [[Graveyard]] during a [[Spooky Festival]], or from [[Trick or Treat Chest|Trick or Treat Chests]]. It is similar to the other Spooky Mobs, where it only takes 1 [[Damage]] each hit. If it is attacked with a [[Ghoul Buster]], it takes 2 [[Damage]] per hit.
## Spawning
The Scary Jerry can naturally spawn in the [[Graveyard]] and [[Wilderness]] at night while a [[Spooky Festival]] is active. It can also be spawned from TRICK [[Trick or Treat Chest|Trick or Treat Chests]]. It has a 3.06122449% chance to be the Mob spawned by the Trick or Treat Chest.

# Scatha
Aliases: Scappa
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Confirmations needed, Crystal Hollows
[Scatha]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 181
Spawn Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Mob Type: [[Subterranean]] [[Shielded]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 10 (hits); [[Damage]]: 2,500
[Drops]
Drops: 4x [[Worm Membrane]] 1x Fine Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x [[Dwarven O's Block Bran]] 0–1x Fine Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x Fine Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x RARE Scatha Pet ([[Scatha Pet]]) 0–1x EPIC Scatha Pet ([[Scatha Pet]]) 0–1x LEGENDARY Scatha Pet ([[Scatha Pet]]) 0–1x Fine Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x Fine Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
XP: +271 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 199
[Metadata]
ID: SCATHA_10
A Scatha is a mob that has a chance to spawn when mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
A [[Stoneworm]] or Scatha has a 0.167% (1 in 600) chance to spawn mining [[Hard Stone]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. There is an 80% (4 in 5) chance for the spawned mob to be a [[Stoneworm]], and a 20% (1 in 5) chance for a Scatha to spawn. There is a 30s cooldown after a Scatha or [[Stoneworm]] spawns.
Chance to spawn [[Stoneworm|Stoneworms]] can be increased with [[Reforging|Blazing]] reforge, [[Heart of the Mountain|Tunnel Vision HotM]] perk and [[Marigold|Treasure of the Earth Gold Essence Shop]] perk. Chance to replace [[Stoneworm|Stoneworms]] with [[Scatha]] Scathas can be increased with [[Tracking]].
The [[Armadillo Pet]] and Mole perk do not prevent spawning by themselves, however the conditions mentioned above must still be met.
After digging out a suitable tunnel, breaking the blocks at the player's eye level on the side can still trigger Scathas spawning. Breaking the blocks below foot level will also spawn Scathas.
While spawning, Scathas make a unique squelching sound 3 times, alerting players of their presence. Additionally, the following message appears in chat:
You hear the sound of something approaching...
## Behavior
A Scatha slowly slithers through the tunnel it spawned in, turning back if it hits a block or gets too far away. It has 10 [[Health]]. They start off with a chain of 10 segments, which decrease by one each time they are hit until killed. Each hit deals 1 of damage to the Scatha.
If no blocks are present under the Scatha when it moves, the Scatha will float through the air. It does not go up or down and stays true to the Y-Axis it spawns at.

- Scatha can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Scatha Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.
- A [[Nothing-in-a-Bottle]] can be used on a Scatha to turn it into a [[Scatha-in-a-Bottle]]. This does not count as a kill and therefore also doesn't trigger the usual drops.
- The drop chances are not reduced to 1/5th from Loot Sharing.

## Trivia
- Scatha is a reference to the Scatha from The Lord of the Rings.
- Scathas and [[Stoneworm|Stoneworms]] are affected by [[Derpy|Mayor Derpy]]'s DOUBLE MOB HP!!! perk.
- Players must hit the head of a Scatha at least once in order to be eligible for loot share. Hitting another part does not count.
- When loot sharing, at most 1 [[Scatha Pet]] used to be able to drop among all loot share contributors. This limit doesn't exist anymore since presumably the October 5th 2026 update, which might be unintended.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- [[Huntaxes]] do not work on Scathas, presumably due to them being hit-based instead of using regular Health.

## Drops

### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv5]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Scribe Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Behavior
Scribes are passive until a player damages them. When hostile, they move towards the player and take 5 [[Rift Time]] from the player on contact. After taking 3 hits from the player, they will become invulnerable, rise into the air, and create a Beam extending from their eyes. While in this state, they will use the Beam by turn blocks under them into Block of Coal, then begin following the player with the Beam. The player can turn the Blocks of Coal into Block of Gold by walking over or looking at them. If the player is hit by a Beam, they will lose 20s [[Rift Time]]. After all of the Blocks of Coal are turned into Blocks of Gold, Scribes will descend back to the ground and become vulnerable again.

# Shadow

The Shadow is a mob that spawns in the [[West Village]]. It appears as a floating head.
## Drops
[[Shadow Crux]] drops are affected by the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades.
Item drops - [Lv3]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|

|[[Shadow Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv3]]

|[[Motes]]|150|150|150|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv3]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Shadow Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Behavior
Shadows are passive until a player damages them. When hostile, they move towards the player and take 5 [[Rift Time]] from the player on contact. After taking 3 hits from the player, they will become invulnerable and move into the ground, following the player. While in this state, they will use the Shadow Bump ability. If the player is hit, they will lose 60 [[Rift Time]]. After they use it 3 times, they will move back out of the ground and the player can kill them.
## Trivia
- The [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]] will anger nearby Shadows.

# Shiny Pig
Aliases: Harvesting 6, Shiny Pigs
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, The Uprising, Special items
[Shiny Pig]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 157
Spawn Location: [[Village]]
Mob Type: [[Animal]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000
[Drops]
XP: +235 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 172
[Metadata]
ID: SHINY_PIG_1
Shiny Pigs are mobs that spawn around the [[Village]] during the [[Year of the Pig]]. They can be clicked on with [[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]] to receive various [[Shiny Orb|rewards]] after returning them to the orbs.
## Catching
Clicking a Shiny Pig while holding a [[Shiny Orb]] launches the pig a short distance away while the orb hovers in place where it was used. The Shiny Pig must be returned to its associated orb by hitting it or making it walk there (it will walk away from the player who owns it). Once done, the Shiny Pig disappears and charges the orb, allowing it to be clicked for a reward. Only the player that used the orb may claim a prize from it.
A Shiny Pig that goes 90 seconds without returning to its associated [[Shiny Orb]] will despawn alongside the orb without giving any loot. The [[Shiny Orb]] will not be refunded.
A single player may own up to 2 Shiny Pigs at once, but each will only charge the specific [[Shiny Orb]] they were clicked with.

## Tips
- It's recommended to use the [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]] or a [[Shiny Rod]] to lead Shiny Pigs to [[Shiny Orb|Shiny Orbs]] more effectively.
  - Alternatively, the player can use a high [[Attack Speed]] weapon, such as a [[Zombie Soldier Cutlass]], to allow themselves to hit the Shiny Pig farther.
- It is also possible to use Knockback Displacement to redirect Shiny Pigs, though quite difficult.
## Bugs
- Shiny Pigs can occasionally phase through blocks and fly to sections of the map that are unreachable when clicked with a [[Shiny Orb]].
- The two side arrows from the [[Runaan's Bow]] target Shiny Pigs.
## Trivia
- When Shiny Pigs were first added, they were able to be killed with the [[Aspect of the Dragons]]'s ability.

# Shrieky Tiki
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Article stubs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Shrieky Tiki]

A Shrieky Tiki is a [[Mob]] that can be found in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Torrhus Heights]].
## Spawning
Shrieky Tikis spawn as a totem of heads in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Torrhus Heights]]. When left-clicked, the clicked head and the one above it (or the bottom one, if the top one is clicked), will rotate clockwise from the head-down point of view, and the opposite direction when right-clicked. If the top 2 heads are facing the same direction, they will stop rotating. When all three are facing the same direction, the Shrieky Tiki awakens and attacks the player.

# Shy

Mob Type: None

ID: SHAME_1
The Shy is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Wyld Woods]]. It appears as a floating head.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Shy Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|100|100|100|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
## Behavior
Shys attack the player through close exposure and reduce their [[Rift Time]] by 4s. After taking few hits from the player, they will become momentarily invulnerable, then say one of their possible lines, and, after a short delay, attack the player for 60 [[Rift Time]], unless the player looks away from the Shy.
### Possible Lines
- Don't look at me!
- Look away!
- Eek!
- I'm ugly! :(
## Trivia
- Shys are the only enemy inside of the [[Rift Dimension]] with dialogue.
- The internal name for Shy is Shame, suggesting that this was the original name for the mob before the current one.

A Silvo is a [[Mob]] that spawns as a projectile from [[Grunt|Grunts]], a [[Team Treasurite]] member found in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Silvos can be spawned when fighting [[Grunt|Grunts]]. After a few seconds, it will despawn, before the Grunt spawns another one again.
## Behavior
Silvos will slowly crawl towards the player it was shot towards to attack them. It despawns after a few seconds.

## Tips
- Since Slimey Slimes can spawn in light, creating a lit up platform below Y level 30 prevents reduces the chance of other [[Mob|Mobs]] spawning instead.
- A [[Weather Node]] or [[Weather Wand]] can be used to create rain to spawn Slimey Slimes easily.
## Trivia
- Slimey Slimes are a reference to the Blitz Survival Advanced Kit, which shares the same name.
SlimeySlime I Advanced Kit: [[Slimeball]]

A Slug is a [[Pests|Garden Pest]]. Its corresponding crop is [[Mushroom]]. It is attracted to [[Plant Matter]].
## Spawning
Slugs can spawn in the [[The Garden]], starting at [[The Garden|Garden IX (9)]].
## Behavior
Slugs slowly run away from the player. Slugs can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]] or [[Lassos]].

A Smog is a [[Mob]] that spawns as a projectile from [[Boss Corleone]], a [[Team Treasurite]] member found rarely in specific rooms of [[Mithril Deposits]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Smogs can be spawned when fighting [[Boss Corleone]]. After a few seconds, it will despawn, before another one gets spawned again.
## Behavior
Smogs will shoot wither skulls at the player, then explode and despawn after a few seconds, dealing damage to nearby players.

Drops: 1x [[Gunpowder]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Gunpowder]] 0–1x [[Exp Share Core]]
XP: +7 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 5
[Metadata]
ID: INVISIBLE_CREEPER_3
Sneaky Creepers are a type of Creeper that spawn exclusively in the [[Gunpowder Mines]]. They have the appearance of a normal creeper - however, they are invisible until they approach the player.
## Behavior
The Sneaky Creeper spawns in the Gunpowder Mines completely invisible.
Upon getting in range of a player, the Sneaky Creeper will become visible. It will then also begin to explode, like a normal creeper. If the player runs away before it explodes, it will stay visible, and act entirely like a normal creeper. Killing the Sneaky Creeper before it explodes will prevent the explosion, granting [[Combat]] XP and some [[Gunpowder]].

## Strategy
Sneaky Creepers are not really a threat, but it is hard to kill them without them exploding.
There are some strategies to make it easier:
- Using a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Skeleton Pet]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- [[Arrows|Bouncy Arrows]] can be used to make it easier to get multiple in a row.
- [[Frozen Blaze Armor]] kills nearby sneaky creepers with its damaging aura
- Wearing a [[Vampire Witch Mask]]
- [[Aurora Staff]]
- Enable show supporting blocks option.
## Trivia
- Sneaky Creepers have a [[Drop Chance|9Pray RNGesus]] chance to drop an [[Exp Share Core]]. If the player gets this early on, they can get a bit of money.
- They can be killed by homing weapon when invisible which allows them to be farmed easily for bestiary.

A Sneaky Tiki is a [[Mob]] that can be found in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Torrhus Heights]].
## Spawning
Sneaky Tikis spawn as a totem of heads in the [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Torrhus Heights]]. When left-clicked, the clicked head and the one above it (or the bottom one, if the top one is clicked), will rotate clockwise from the head-down point of view, and the opposite direction when right-clicked. If the top 2 heads are facing the same direction, they will stop rotating. When all three are facing the same direction, the Sneaky Tiki awakens and attacks the player.

# Soul Dragon
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[Soul Dragon]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 765
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]; [[Ender]]; [[Airborne]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000,000,000; [[Defense]]: 2,500; [[Damage]]: 720,000
[Drops]
XP: +1,147 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 841
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_WITHER_KING_DRAGON_100
The Soul Dragon, also named Withered Dragon, is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the final phase of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Mode Catacombs - Floor VII]] boss fight.
## Boss Fight
The bottom of Master Mode Floor VII contains a giant throne room. There are five Relics placed next to Dragon statues around the room. There are also five cauldrons in front of the throne near the center of the room. They each have a specifically colored Clay below them. The players must bring and place the Relics with the same corresponding color as the Cauldron's block of clay. By doing so, The Wither King will be summoned on his throne.
Damage cannot be dealt towards The Wither King, and he mostly acts as a spectator to the battle. The Wither King spawns five dragons with special abilities, including the Soul Dragon. All five dragons must be killed near their corresponding colored Dragon statue to destroy them. All Dragons can respawn at any time indefinitely until all dragon statues are destroyed. If all five statues are destroyed, the floor is completed.
## Behavior
The Soul Dragon does not fly around, and will sometimes teleport to another spot in the throne room. It teleports to its statue every 3 times it teleports.

A Special Zealot is a [[Mob]] that has a chance to spawn when the Player kills a [[Zealot]] or [[Zealot Bruiser]].
## Spawning
When a Player kills a normal [[Zealot]], there is a base 0.2381% (1 in 420) chance for a Special Zealot to spawn nearby.
When a Special Zealot spawns, the Player who contributed to its spawn will hear a Wither sound effect and the following chat message:
A special Zealot has spawned nearby!
#### Increasing Spawn Chances
Multiple factors can increase the chance of a Special Zealot spawning:

|-|-|-|
|[[Zealot Bruiser]]|×1.105|1/380|
|[Lv100] LEGENDARY Enderman Pet|×1.25|1/336|
|Zealuck ([[Essence Shops]]) Ⅰ|×1.02|1/411.76|
|Zealuck ([[Essence Shops]]) Ⅱ|×1.04|1/403.85|
|Zealuck ([[Essence Shops]]) Ⅲ|×1.06|1/396.23|

|Pity ([[Pity and RNG Meters]])|Varies|Varies|

The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]]. Since this mob is [[Elusive]], [[Charm Chance|Charming]] is guaranteed to proc on kill.

The Player who contributed to the spawn of the Special Zealot will always get the [[Summoning Eye]] once the Special Zealot dies, even if another player killed it. However only the killer receives the shard.

The Spider Jockey is a type of Spider carrying a [[Jockey Skeleton]]. They can appear in the [[Spider Mound]] and [[Arachne's Burrow]].
## Spawning
The lower level Spider Jockey appears in the center of the [[Spider Mound]], mostly on the higher levels. The higher level variant spawns in the [[Arachne's Burrow]].
## Behavior
The Spider Jockey runs and jumps towards the player, dealing damage on contact. The [[Jockey Skeleton]] shoots [[Silverfish (Jockey Skeleton)|Silverfish]] at the player.

# Splatter
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Rift Dimension
[Splatter]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 6
Spawn Location: [[Stillgore Château]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 160; [[Rift Damage]]: 6
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Splatter Crux]]
XP: +40 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: SPLATTER_6
The Splatter is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Stillgore Château]]. It appears as a floating head.
## Drops
[[Splatter Crux]] drops are affected by the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades.
Item drops - [Lv6]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Splatter Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv6]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|150|150|150|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|40|40|40|
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv6]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Splatter Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Behavior
Splatters are passive until a player damages them or if another Splatter applied Splattered Hearts to the player. When hostile, they move towards the player and take 6s [[Rift Time]] and 1 [[Hearts]] from the player on contact. After taking 3 hits from the player, they will temporarily apply Splattered Hearts to the player, reducing their maximum [[Hearts]] by 8 and creating 4 spots around the player that can be stepped on to regain [[Hearts]] back. Each spot will give the player 2 [[Hearts]] back.
Nearby Splatters turn hostile toward players who did not collect all their [[Hearts]] back.
Splatters can be instantly killed by the [[Steak Stake]] when below 20% [[Health]] .
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Splatter.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Splatter.|
|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Splatter now gives 40 [[Combat]] XP.|

Splitter Spiders spawn in the [[Spider Mound]] and [[Arachne's Burrow]] sections of the [[Spider's Den]].

Splitter Spiders will stand still until a player is nearby. They will jump towards the player and deal [[Damage]] on contact. Upon death, they will spawn 2 [[Silverfish (Splitter Spider)|Silverfish]].

## Strategy
- Using the [[Golem Sword]] to kill the spider and then using its ability to kill the silverfish once they spawn from its corpse will make the Silverfish much easier to kill.
- Despite their relatively low [[Health]], being knocked off an edge by the sudden extra silverfish spawns on their death can be a death sentence for new players. Consider using the [[Slime Hat]] for anti-knockback if they are giving you too much trouble.
- Using an [[Intimidation Talisman]] will help so that the silverfish won't attack you after they spawn. This does not work for the higher-level Splitter Spiders that spawn in the secondary cave (where [[Arachne's Keeper|Arachne's Keepers]] spawn), as despite only having 50 health, the Silverfish that spawn there are Level 42.
- The [[Flower of Truth]]'s ability, if aimed at a Splitter Spider, can kill the two resulting silverfish instantly.
## Trivia
- Upon killing a Splitter Spider, the screen will display an 'Armor Stand' tag for a 1-2 frames before disappearing. This is merely a glitch and doesn't affect the player or the resulting Silverfish spawns.

# Star Sentry
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Dwarven Mines
[Star Sentry]

[Drops]
Drops: 5x [[Mithril]] 0–1x [[Starfall]]
XP: +202 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 148
[Metadata]
ID: CRYSTAL_SENTRY_50
The Star Sentry is a [[Mob]] that spawns near [[Fallen Stars]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Spawning
Star Sentries are spawned by the [[Fallen Stars|Fallen Star]] Event, which spawns at some of the sub-locations of the [[Dwarven Mines]]:
- [[Royal Mines]]
- [[Far Reserve]]
- [[Goblin Burrows]]
- [[Upper Mines]]
- [[Rampart's Quarry]]
- [[Great Ice Wall]]
- [[Cliffside Veins]]
## Behavior
The Star Sentry takes 1 [[Damage]] per hit, regardless of the Accessories, Weapons, or Armor used. They do not take [[Damage]] from a weapon's [[Thunderlord]] [[Enchantments|Enchantment]], requiring the Star Sentry to be hit 10 times.
While idle, they will wander around the Fallen Star they were spawned by, only attacking and following the player if they are within 5 blocks of them. They are quick to lose aggression if outside that range.
## Drops
Unlike regular mobs, Star Sentries drop their item drops on hit, not on kill.
Item drops - [Lv135]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mithril]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
|[[Starfall]]|1|4Uncommon Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|20%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv135]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|45|45|45|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|202|202|202|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|148|148|148|
Star Sentry can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Star Sentry Shard]] and +300 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+3,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

# Stoneworm
Aliases: Hard Stone Worm, Stone Worm, Worm
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Stoneworm]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 119
Spawn Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Mob Type: [[Subterranean]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5; [[Damage]]: 400
[Drops]
Drops: 1x Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 1x [[Worm Membrane]] 0–1x Rough Amethyst Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x Rough Amber Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) 0–1x Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
XP: +178 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 130
[Metadata]
ID: WORM_5
A Stoneworm is a [[Mob]] that can spawn when mining [[Hard Stone]] inside the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
A Stoneworm or [[Scatha]] has a 0.167% (1 in 600) chance of spawning when breaking [[Hard Stone]] in a tunnel within the [[Crystal Hollows]]. There is a 30s cooldown every time a Stoneworm or [[Scatha]] spawns. A Stoneworm has an 80% (4 in 5) chance of being spawned, while a [[Scatha]] has a 20% (1 in 5) chance of being spawned instead.
Chance to spawn [[Stoneworm]] Stoneworms can be increased with [[Reforging|Blazing]] reforge, [[Heart of the Mountain|Tunnel Vision HotM]] perk and [[Marigold|Treasure of the Earth Gold Essence Shop]] perk. Chance to replace [[Stoneworm]] Stoneworms with [[Scatha|Scathas]] can be increased with [[Tracking]].
While spawning, Stoneworms and [[Scatha|Scathas]] make a unique squelching sound 3 times, alerting players of their presence. Additionally, the following message appears in chat:
You hear the sound of something approaching...
## Behavior
A Stoneworm slowly slithers through the tunnel it spawned in, turning back if it hits a block or gets too far away. It has 5 [[Health]]. They start off with a chain of 5 segments, which decrease by one each time they are hit until killed. Each hit deals 1 of damage to the Stoneworm.
If no blocks are present under the Stoneworm when it moves, it will float through the air. It does not go up or down and stays at the Y level it spawned at.

## Tips
- While [[Mining Spread]] does not directly prevent the player from spawning Stoneworms and [[Scatha|Scathas]], it may reduce the chances depending on how the player mines.
  - Generally, it is recommended to turn off all sources of [[Mining Spread]] while digging a tunnel forwards, and then mine the ceiling or wall of the tunnel backwards with [[Mining Spread]] enabled to maximize spawn chances.
- A 1×1 tunnel being crawled through does not count as a valid tunnel for worms to spawn. It must be a minimum of two blocks tall.
## Trivia
- Stoneworms and [[Scatha|Scathas]] are affected by [[Derpy|Mayor Derpy]]'s DOUBLE MOBS HP!!! perk.

# Storm
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Pages with UIs, More info needed, Catacombs, Dungeons, Bosses, Dungeon Mobs
[Storm]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 671-1,147
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Description: The Wither Lords; Disciples of the Wither King. Inherited the Catacombs eons ago. Never defeated, feared by anything living AND dead.
Mob Type: [[Wither]] [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 400,000,000-1,000,000,000; [[Defense]]: 1,800 (Master Mode); [[Damage]]: 7,200-720,000
[Metadata]
ID: STORM_1; MASTER_STORM_1
Storm is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] along with [[Maxor]], [[Goldor]] and [[Necron]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]] in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] and is a disciple of [[The Wither King]].
## Collection
Killing Storm and ALL the other Withers increases the Necron collection.
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|50|[[Wither Blood]] Reward|+15|
|II|100|Golden Necron Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|III|150|Wither Helmet ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+15|
|IV|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|V|500|Wither Leggings ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VI|750|Wither Chestplate ([[Wither Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|VII|1,000|Diamond Necron Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
## Boss Fight
The boss fight is split into four phases. (Five phases in Master Mode.) There are four Wither Lords: Maxor, Storm, Goldor and Necron. Each Wither will be a boss for a phase that must be killed individually for each phase.
In all parts of the boss fight, lava damages the player for 20% of their max health, as well as launching them upwards in one of 2 ways:
- Regular: You are launched into the air at a standard height ([More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) blocks).
- Super: Looking upwards at a pitch of -40 or lower triggers a Super lava bounce, boosting you 30% higher than a Regular jump.
### Phase 2
The factory's second layer is exposed. Storm will now fight after Maxor was defeated in Phase 1. This layer comprises a large hall; a main platform with 4 diorite crushers, an outer ring with control pads for the crushers in the corner, and a ring of lava in between. Wither Miners and Withermancers cover the area, with Wither Guards located on raised platforms. One 20M [[Shadow Assassin]] guards each control pad. Storm will attempt to damage the player with lightning bolts within an area from fireballs thrown onto the ground. Storm will occasionally start a countdown, after which lightning will strike anywhere that is not under an intact crusher and deal 2 bursts of 75% of player's [[Health]] as true damage (Normal Mode), which can one-shot most players. Anything which keeps you alive after taking a fatal hit, such as a [[Wither Cloak Sword]], can negate the damage (except on Master Mode).
Standing on the control pads raises and lowers the corresponding crusher (except the red crusher, which is broken). When Storm is crushed by a pillar, he will be stunned and become vulnerable for 10 seconds. After this or when his health is drained to 50%, he will enter an enraged state.
The next phase will begin when Storm has been crushed under at least two pillars (purple, green or yellow), and enough damage has been dealt to Storm. Failing to deal enough damage after crushing Storm with all three pillars softlocks the fight (but the amount of damage Storm deals is enough to kill all players.)

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|When entering Phase 2|[BOSS] Storm: Pathetic Maxor, just like expected. [BOSS] Storm: Don't boast about beating this simple minded Wither. [BOSS] Storm: My abilities are unparalleled, in many ways I am the last bastion. [BOSS] Storm: The memory of your death will be your fondest, focus up!|
|When about to start Thunder Strike cooldown|[BOSS] Storm: The power of lightning is quite phenomenal. A single strike can vaporize a person whole. [BOSS] Storm: I'd be happy to show you what that's like!|
|When using Thunder Strike|[BOSS] Storm: THUNDER LET ME BE YOUR CATALYST! [BOSS] Storm: ENERGY HEED MY CALL!|
|When 3 Pillars are Destroyed|[BOSS] Storm: Bahahaha! Not a single intact pillar remains! [BOSS] Storm: Rejoice, your last moments are with me and my lightning.|
|When killed|[BOSS] Storm: I should have known that I stood no chance. [BOSS] Storm: At least my son died by your hands.|
|If all players die|[BOSS] Storm: FINALLY! This took way too long. [BOSS] Storm: Now that you're a Ghost, can you help me clean up?|
|Condition|Random Dialogue|
|None|[BOSS] Storm: The Age of Men is over, we are creating tens, hundreds of withers!!|
|None|[BOSS] Storm: Not just your land, but every kingdom will soon be ruled by our army of undead!|
|None|[BOSS] Storm: The days are numbered until I am finally unleashed again on the world!|
|None|[BOSS] Storm: No more adventurers, no more heroes, death and thunder!|
|If the player is under a pillar|[BOSS] Storm: Fool, I'd hide under something next time if I were you!|

|When Crushed by a pillar|[BOSS] Storm: Oof|
|When Crushed by a pillar|[BOSS] Storm: Ouch, that hurt!|
|When a Pillar is Destroyed|[BOSS] Storm: THAT WAS ONLY IN MY WAY!|
|When a Pillar is Destroyed|[BOSS] Storm: This factory is too small for me!|
|When a Pillar is Destroyed|[BOSS] Storm: Slowing me down will be your greatest accomplishment!|
## Lore
- According to a journal, [[Journal|The Father]], Sadan is Storm's son. And the reason Sadan decided to develop a technique that would defeat a Wither, however it is unknown if the Wither in question was Storm.
- Based on the dialogue of [[The Wither King]], Storm was his favorite Sparring partner.
- To gain his third head, Storm absorbed [[Solomon]], a very powerful human mage, sent with the expedition to [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
- After humans discovered The Catacombs and sent an expedition there, which has not returned, Storm was sent by Necron to destroy the Human castle, which has survived both [[Jythar|Calamity]] and [[The Wither King|Kaeman's]] hunt for vengeance.
## Trivia
- Storm seems to be the most egoistic of the five Withers, a trait he shares with his son [[Sadan]]. They both however seem to make up with each other when faced with death, as their last words reference each other.
- Storm was going to be the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor IX]] before Hypixel removed [[Maxor]], [[Goldor]] and Storm from the game and replaced them with [[Necron]].
  - All three bosses were later re-added to The Catacombs - Floor VII.
- [[Storm's Armor]] and [[Storm the Fish]] are references to this boss.
- Luring Storm into the red pad area softlocks the fight. It is unknown if this glitch is still present.

# Stridersurfer

Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Fishing, Sea Creatures

A Stridersurfer is a type of [[Mob]] found in the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]]. It is also a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up there.
## Spawning
[Lv58] Stridersurfers spawn in the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]], while [Lv62] ones can be fished up as a [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]].
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for [Lv62] Stridersurfer is 200, resulting in a default catch chance of 100% (1 in 1 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase its weight:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
(Note that this weight modification will have no effect in practice, since it is the only Sea Creature that can be caught in this pool.)
### Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Fishing up|You caught a Stridersurfer.|
## Behavior
Stridersurfers walk around in the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]]. On death, they drop 1 [[Gill Membrane]]: they also have a chance to drop [[Sturdy Bone|Sturdy Bones]]. They can also be hunted to obtain the [[Stridersurfer Shard]], which can be syphoned to grant the [[Attributes|Magmatic Ruler]] attribute.

# Sun Gecko
Aliases: Gecko, Sun
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Article stubs, Pages with UIs
[Sun Gecko]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 20
Spawn Location: [[Time Chamber]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 250
[Drops]
Drops: 5x [[Sun Gecko Essence]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: SUN_GECKO_20
Sun Gecko is a [[Bosses|BOSS]] in the [[Time Chamber]], accessed by talking with [[Oruo The Almighty]] in the [[Continuum]]. Each fight grants 5 [[Sun Gecko Essence]] and 50 Sun Gecko Experience initially, but can be increased with Time Chamber Modifiers.
While fighting Sun Gecko, [[Rift Time]] drains twice as fast.
## Time Chamber Modifiers
This article needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Needs reformatting to match ingame text 1:1
See more stubs.
Each modifier grants an additional 5 [[Sun Gecko Essence]] and 50 Sun Gecko Experience.
|Icon|Modifier|Description|Advanced Tips|
|-|-|-|-|
|Revival|Revival|Shurikens are now placed around the Chamber. Sun Gecko now revives once when reaching 0 Health.|Shurikens can attack Sun Gecko from a distance, meaning you can keep your combo up from far away. When Sun Gecko revives, you keep your Combo Multiplier! Make sure you rush back to him to keep it up!|
|Combo Maniac|Combo Maniac|Increasing your Combo Multiplier makes your next attack stun Sun Gecko. Losing your Combo against Sun Gecko will immediately deal 4 [[Hearts]] of damage.|Stunning Sun Gecko will prevent him from attacking for about 0.7s. Regardless of this modifier, keeping up your Combo is the most important aspect of fighting Sun Gecko.|
|Time Sliced|Time Sliced|For the first 30 seconds of the fight, your Combo Multiplier requires one less hit to increases. You have 3 minutes to defeat Sun Gecko. For every second after, take increasing amounts of [[Hearts]] damage.|If you find yourself being Time Sliced, some items may help you stay alive slightly longer. You can take hits early in the fight to take better advantage of the easier Combo Multiplier.|
|Buffantics|Buffantics|Various traps, hazards, and buffs are placed around the Chamber.|The Combo Buff also resets the Combo Timer, meaning that you can run away to pick it up!|
|Collective|Collective|For every modifier enabled, lose -1 Max [[Hearts]] at the start of the fight. For every modifier enabled, increase the time before you lose your Combo by 0.2 seconds.|Even though you lose hearts from this modifier, heart increases from other sources will still apply! All healing in the Time Chamber is reduced by 60%, regardless of this modifier. Heal often!|
|Brand New Dance|Brand New Dance|Sun Gecko gains a new attack, and some other attacks are buffed and can now deal [[Hearts]] damage. When taking [[Hearts]] damage, regenerate 10 Mana.|Be careful of Wyld Nuke, make sure to maintain your combo by keeping Sun Gecko close, with Shurikens or with Combo Buff. Spirit Beam hitbox becomes much larger.|
|Culmination|Culmination|‎ [[Hearts]] damage cannot be healed in any way. Your Combo Multiplier requires one less hit to increase.|Don't get hit.|
## Attacks
|Attack Name|Attack Description|
|-|-|
|Instant Transmission|Sun Gecko teleports to your location.|
|Perfect Volley|Sun Gecko sends you up into the sky flying in a random direction.|
|Extreme Speed|Sun Gecko spawns 3 clones of himself which cannot take damage. Deals [[Hearts]] damage while Brand New Dance is active.|
|Spirit Beam|Sun Gecko flies into the sky and casts a beam down that removes 1s [[Rift Time]] . Deals damage to [[Hearts]] when using Brand New Dance.|
|Wyld Nuke|Sun Gecko spawns a nuke, which can be disarmed with left-click. This attack is only active while Brand New Dance is active.|

## Trivia
- Sun Gecko most likely is a reference to Son Goku from the Dragon Ball franchise.
  - Sun Gecko could also reference the historic Chinese military strategist, Sun Tzu.
- Beating Sun Gecko for the first time rewards the [[Achievements|"If you wish to defeat me..."]] Achievement.
  - This achievement most likely originates from a phrase from Technoblade's video "Skeppy vs Technoblade: The Final Reckoning".
- Beating Sun Gecko with all 7 Time Chamber Modifiers rewards the [[Achievements|Maximum Power]] Achievement.
- [[Extremely Real Shuriken|Extremely Real Shurikens]] can be used as a substitute for [[Time Shuriken|Time Shurikens]] to keep up the player's combo.

The Superprotectron is a mob that spawns during [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raids]], in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Spawning
Superprotectrons spawn during the [[Goblin Raid]] event in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Behavior
If a player is near it, a Superprotectron will attack the player, dealing 50 [[Damage]] and [[Knockback]]. The Superprotectron's ability causes all [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblins]] in the immediate vicinity to take less melee damage and unable to take ranged damage or ability damage. Killing the Superprotectron will also remove the protection for the surrounding mobs, allowing them to take the full amount of damage inflicted. Upon death, it will contribute 10 kills to each player who damaged it.

# Sven Alpha
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Slayer, Wolf Slayer, Minibosses
[Sven Alpha]
Type: [[Mob]]

[Drops]

[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 714
[Metadata]
ID: SVEN_FOLLOWER_4_BOOSTED
The Sven Alpha is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]] that can be spawned during [[Sven Packmaster]] Tier IV Quests.
## Spawning
Sven Alphas spawn rarely during [[Sven Packmaster]] Tier IV quests when the player kills a Wolf-type mob. When this happens, an explosion effect takes place and the Sven Alpha spawns.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv238]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Wolf Tooth]]|5|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv238]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|1,785|1,785|1,785|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|714|714|714|
## Behavior
The Sven Alpha seeks out and attacks nearby players. It moves more slowly than its cousin the [[Pack Enforcer]], and tends to stay within close proximity of its spawning location. If it wanders outside that radius, it will become docile, ceasing to attack players. It has 480k [[Health]] and deals 2,200 [[Damage]] per hit.
If it has not been defeated after a while, it will despawn.
## Strategy
- Players can choose to fight the miniboss in a body of water as, like the Sven Packmaster and the other wolves, it is slowed down and players can control the damage that it deals to them.
## Trivia
- The Sven Alpha does more [[Damage]] than the [[Sven Packmaster]] itself unless the latter lands a critical hit.
- The Sven Alpha's name is from PewDiePie's Minecraft dog, named Sven. The Alpha part comes from the concept of Alpha Wolves.

Rabid Wolf genetically modified by a famous mad scientist. Eats bones and flesh.
— Maddox the Slayer
Sven Packmaster is a [[Slayer]] [[Bosses|BOSS]] in the [[Wolf Slayer]] branch. It is an agile Wolf that deals [[True Damage]].
Defeating the Tier IV Sven Packmaster results in unlocking the [[Enderman Slayer]] quest and the [[Voidgloom Seraph]] boss.

## Behavior
The Sven Packmaster is a Wolf-type mob which is hostile towards all players. It is very fast and has a melee attack.
As with all Slayer bosses, if the Sven Packmaster has not dealt damage to a player for some time, it will begin rapidly regenerating health. The regeneration immediately stops when it hits a player again. Additionally, if either it or its target stays in the same block for too long, it performs a slam-dunk attack, knocking the target towards it and dealing [[True Damage]]. If 4 minutes pass without the boss being defeated, it will despawn, failing the Slayer quest. A timer is shown above its head indicating the remaining time.
Like the Revenant Horror, if the Sven Packmaster enters the village it will immediately despawn and you will fail the Slayer Quest.
Each tier has all of the abilities from the one before it.
|Tier|[[Health]]|[[Damage]]|[[True Damage]]|Ability|Slayer XP Reward|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|I|2k|60||Agile The wolf is small and fast, making it harder to hit.|5 XP|
|II|40k|80|10|True Damage Ignores your defense. Very painful.|25 XP|
|III|750k|180|50|Call the pups! At 50% health, it calls its deadly pack of pups.|100 XP|
|IV|2M|700|440|Call the pups! At 50% health, it calls its deadly pack of pups.|500 XP|
### Tier III and Tier IV
Upon reaching 50% health, the Sven Packmaster will begin moving very slowly, and no longer regenerates health[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]; critical particles appear around it. It spawns 1 Sven Pup at its location every second for 5 seconds, during which it is protected from attacks. Each Sven Pup has 10% of the max health of the Packmaster. When it is spawning pups, the text above its head reads "Calling the pups!", after which it says "Protected". If all the pups are killed, the Packmaster returns to its original behavior. When either the pups or the Packmaster attacked the player, the Packmaster won't regenerate health.
## Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 12.5% (1 in 8) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.
|Mob|Stats|Spawned at/via|Mob Types|Item Drops|Non-Item Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv109] [[Pack Enforcer]]|[[Health]]: 45,000; [[Damage]]: 360|Tier III [[Wolf Slayer]]|[[Animal]]|1x [[Wolf Tooth]]|+817 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 327 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv169] [[Sven Follower]]|[[Health]]: 120,000; [[Damage]]: 1,200|Tier IV [[Wolf Slayer]]|[[Animal]]|2x [[Wolf Tooth]]|+1,267 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 507 [[Experience]]|
|[Lv238] [[Sven Alpha]]|[[Health]]: 480,000; [[Damage]]: 2,200|Tier IV [[Wolf Slayer]]|[[Animal]]|5x [[Wolf Tooth]]|+1,785 Combat XP ([[Combat]]); 1 Coin ([[Coins]]); 714 [[Experience]]|
## Tips
- An [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Wolf Pet]] is especially useful to tank oncoming attacks better, as its Alpha Dog ability can cut down up to 30% of the damage received from the Sven Packmaster.
  - Its Pack Leader ability also increases the [[Player]]'s [[Crit Damage]], which stacks well with [[Mastiff Armor]]'s Absolute Unit ability, which gives more [[Health]] per [[Crit Damage]].
  - Giving the pet a [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]] can be a good idea if the player has a hard time fighting the boss due to being constantly thrown around by its high knockback.
  - The extra [[Health]] from this ability combo helps tank the pups that the Sven Packmaster Tier III and above spawns.
- Sven Packmasters Tier III and above will stop regenerating [[Health]] once it starts spawning its pups, and the boss itself will move very slowly. It is recommended to heal oneself during this time try to not to take any [[Damage]] when it is this state.
- A common strategy for players who cannot tank the Sven Packmaster's attacks well is to fight it in water, which allows the player to control the damage they receive to prevent health regeneration. The pups that spawn with a Sven Packmaster Tier III and above will stop attacking the [[Player]] and drown.
  - The pond next in the [[Mountain]], near the [[Ruins]] is quick and easy to fight the boss in. The [[Player]] can grind out wolves that grant high amounts of XP in the [[Howling Cave]] and then teleport to the [[Ruins]] when there is little XP required to spawn the boss. A [[Lead]] can be used on a [[Wolf]] to bring it to the pond, where the player kill it and spawn the boss.
- A [[Radiant Power Orb]] and its upgrades are recommended for players using [[Mastiff Armor]] to combat the Sven Packmaster.
  - Normal, non-multiplicative regeneration items such as [[Wand of Healing]] and its upgrades become next to useless when using [[Mastiff Armor]] due to it barely adding up to the massive health pool it provides. Thus, a [[Zombie Sword]] and its upgrade, the [[Ornate Zombie Sword]] is a substitute for instant healing.
- Letting the boss hit the [[Player]] every <15 seconds prevents the boss from regenerating.
- Since the boss does [[True Damage]] at higher tiers, which makes [[Defense]] less valuable, armor that grants high amounts of [[Health]], like [[Superior Dragon Armor]], are highly recommended to absorb the blows from the Packmaster. It is also a good idea to enchant armor with [[Growth]], for an extra [[Health]] boost.
  - [[True Protection]] can help slightly in cutting down received [[Damage]] from the Sven Packmaster.
- [[True Defense]] can be a bit of a liability to get in meaningful quantities early on, but good sources include
  - [[Wolf Pet]] for +10 [[True Defense]], or if you are really desperate for more, the [[Mithril Golem Pet]] for +50 [[True Defense]]
  - [[God Potion]] will grant +20 [[True Defense]]
  - A [[Dwarf Turtle Shelmet]] grants +10 [[True Defense]]
  - The [[True Protection]] enchantment grants +5 [[True Defense]]
- Wearing [[Sorrow Armor]] is advised when fighting this boss, since it has enough [[True Defense]] to keep the [[Player]] alive and additional [[Magic Find]], helping the chances of dropping rarer loot.
- A [[Souls Rebound]] used to be able to prevent the Sven Packmaster second phase from occurring and could kill it in one hit with decent gear. This method is no longer available as it has since been patched.
## Trivia
- This mob's name could be a reference to Sven, the pet wolf in PewDiePie's 2019 Minecraft series.
- The "mad scientist" mentioned in the Sven Packmaster's lore could possibly be [[Aura]].
- All Sven Packmasters are immune to all types of ability damage and ranged damage, including the ability of [[Flower of Truth]]. [[Voodoo Doll]] used to be able to damage the Sven Packmaster but it was shadow-patched.

The Tarantula Broodfather is a [[Slayer]] [[Bosses|BOSS]] in the [[Spider Slayer]] branch. It is an agile [[Spider]] that reduces the player's healing capabilities.

## Behavior
The Tarantula Broodfather is a Spider-type mob hostile to all players. It has the appearance of a cave spider riding a spider. It is agile, able to move quickly, climb walls and jump huge distances. It deals melee damage. Often, it will change targets to other nearby players.
All Tarantula Broodfathers are immune to the abilities of [[Aspect of the Dragons]] and [[Ice Spray Wand]].
As with all Slayer bosses, if the Tarantula Broodfather has not dealt damage to a player for some time, it will begin rapidly regenerating health. The regeneration immediately stops when it hits a player again. Additionally, if either it or its target stays in the same block for too long, it performs a slam-dunk attack, knocking the target towards it and dealing [[True Damage]]. If 3 minutes pass without the boss being defeated, it will despawn, failing the Slayer quest. A timer is shown above its head indicating the remaining time.
Each tier has all of the abilities from the ones before it.
### Tier I
- Backstab - The Broodfather will occasionally leap behind the player and inflict 20% more [[Damage]].
### Tier II
- Backstab - The Broodfather will occasionally leap behind the player and inflict 30% more [[Damage]].
- Noxious Paralysis - The Broodfather reduces the player's [[Vitality]] & [[Speed]] by 15% and 10% respectively when the boss is alive.
### Tier III
- Backstab - The Broodfather will occasionally leap behind the player and inflict 40% more [[Damage]].
- Noxious Paralysis - The Broodfather reduces the player's [[Vitality]] & [[Speed]] by 30% and 25% respectively when the boss is alive.
- Web of Lies - Occurs at 600k (66%) & 300k (33%). The Broodfather will leap to a nearby area and begins spewing web to surround itself. While the Web of Lies is ongoing, it disables teleportation magic unless the player breaks 2 Egg Sacs above the boss with 3 [[Health]] per Egg Sac. If the egg sacs hatch, then they spawn [[Primordial Hatchling|Primordial Hatchlings]] to attack the player.
### Tier IV
- Backstab - The Broodfather will occasionally leap behind the player and inflict 60% more [[Damage]].
- Noxious Paralysis - The Broodfather reduces the player's [[Vitality]] & [[Speed]] by 50% and 50% respectively when the boss is alive.
- Web of Lies - Occurs at 1.6M (66%) & 800k (33%). The player needs to deal with 3 Egg Sacs above the boss with each Egg Sac having 6 [[Health]]. Failing to break one in time will spawn a [[Primordial Hatchling]].
- Bat Barrage - The Broodfather will periodically spawn a horde of [[Primordial Bat|Primordial Bats]] to inflict 0.1% of max [[Health]] as [[Damage]] while becoming immune to Magic Damage.
### Tier V (Primordial Broodfather)
Like the [[Revenant Horror]] Tier V, the Broodfather has a natural regeneration effect and immunity to [[Ability Damage]].
- Backstab - The Broodfather will occasionally leap behind the player and inflict 80% more [[Damage]].
- Noxious Paralysis - The Broodfather reduces the player's [[Vitality]] & [[Speed]] by 75% and 75% respectively when the boss is alive. It also disables [[Absorption]] from all sources.
- Web of Lies - Occurs at 6.7M (66%) & 3.3M (33%). The player must deal with 4 Egg Sacs above the boss with each Egg Sac having 9 [[Health]]. Failing to break one in time will spawn a [[Primordial Minion]].
- Bat Barrage - The Broodfather will periodically spawn a horde of [[Primordial Bat|Primordial Bats]] to inflict 0.2% of max [[Health]] as [[Damage]].
- Till Death Do Us Part - Upon killing the Broodfather, it summons the Primordial Broodmother and fuses to become the Conjoined Brood. Its stats doubles that of [[Tarantula Broodfather]] Tarantula Broodfather Tier V and only retains its passive abilities (Backstab & Noxious Paralysis).
## Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) (Tier IV) and 33.3% (1 in 3) (Tier V) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.

## Tips
- It is recommended to use [[Tarantula Armor]] to kill a Tarantula Broodfather Tier IV with at least 10,000 kills on the armor, as its piece bonus grants higher [[Defense]] against spiders, the more spiders have been killed with the set equipped.
  - Alternatively, a high damaging set such as [[Superior Dragon Armor]] or [[Shadow Assassin Armor]] sets is a sizable improvement over [[Tarantula Armor]] in [[Damage]], and it can decrease the time for each slayer by a measurable amount, due to it focusing on [[Damage]] rather than [[Defense]].
- A [[Radiant Power Orb]] or higher, paired with a [[Spider Hat]] is recommended for fighting Tarantula Broodfather Tier III and above, as an alternative to [[Tarantula Armor]].
- It is highly recommended to use Healing [[Enchantments]] like [[Life Steal]] and [[Vampirism]] on weapons. If the [[Player]]'s [[Health]] gets too low while fighting a Tarantula Broodfather, killing a couple of nearby spiders can activate [[Vampirism]] and keep the [[Player]] alive.
- While fighting the Tarantula Broodfather, backing up into a corner reduces the effect of its leaps and makes it much easier to land hits on the boss.
- If the [[Player]] has a high amount of [[Intelligence]], a [[Voodoo Doll]] can be used to drain the Tarantula Broodfather's [[Health]] quickly.
- It used to be possible to sword-swap between a [[Giant's Sword]] and a [[Scorpion Foil]] to one-shot a Tarantula Broodfather Tier IV. This method is no longer available, however, as since sword-swapping has since been patched.
- While the bats themselves are immune to Magic Damage, the ability from [[Giant's Sword]] counts as melee and it can be used to clear them.
- Since the release of 0.23.5, it is recommended to bring a [[Spider Shortbow]] or better to deal with Egg Sacs at higher tiers.
  - On a [[Ironman|♲ Ironman]] profile, is recommended to grind Tier III bosses until one obtains 3 [[Spider Catalyst]] to craft one, as it is much rarer from higher tiers.
- When dealing with the Tarantula Broodfather Tier V, it is recommended to be aware of multiple [[Arachne's Keeper|Arachne's Keepers]] as the combination of their Venom Shot and the Broodfather's Noxious Paralysis will quickly drain the player's current health.
- Using a [[Tarantula Pet]] and/or [[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula]]/[[Primordial Armor|Primordial]] Chestplate is very useful for combating Noxious Paralysis.
- The best area to spawn the Tarantula Broodfather quickly is the [[Burning Desert]] in the [[Crimson Isle]] by killing [[Flaming Spider|Flaming Spiders]] .

Tarantula Vermin is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|SLAYER MINIBOSS]] for the [[Spider Slayer]].
## Spawning
The Tarantula Vermin spawns rarely when a player kills a [[Spider]] while a [[Tarantula Broodfather]] Tier III+ Slayer quest is active. When this happens, an explosion effect takes place, and the Tarantula Vermin spawns at the location where the spider was killed.
## Behavior
The Tarantula Vermin attempts to leap toward players to damage them on contact.
