# Temporal Pillar
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
[Temporal Pillar]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 100
Spawn Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 100,000
[Metadata]
ID: TEMPORAL_PILLAR_100
The Temporal Pillar is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Village Plaza]].
## Behavior
Temporal Pillar roam around aimlessly. They are surrounded by a circle of particles, which if the player gets too near to, will pull them in. The further in the circle the player is, the harder it is to get out.
If the player gets too close to the enderman for too long, the Minecraft End Poem will appear on screen, the player will be launched up into the air, and their [[Rift Time]] will be reduced to 5, regardless of how much they had before approaching the Temporal Pillar.
## Gallery
- A Temporal Pillar
- A Temporal Pillar with the heads and particles that surround it
## Trivia
- There can only be 1 Temporal Pillar in the [[Village Plaza]] in each lobby.
  - However, there are 3 additional Temporal Pillars in [[Walk of Fame]].
- The Temporal Pillar cannot take damage, and thus cannot be killed in any way.
- If the Temporal Pillar is killed, usually due to exploits or bugs, it drops nothing.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Temporal Pillar.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Temporal Pillar.|
Linked from: [[Achievements]], [[Mob]], [[Mob/List]], [[Village Plaza]], [[Walk of Fame]]

# The Matriarch
Aliases: Matriarch
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Mob pages missing internal IDs, Crimson Isle
[The Matriarch]
Type: [[Mob]]
Spawn Location: [[Matriarch's Lair]]
Mob Type: None
The Matriarch is a mob that can be interacted with to access the [[Belly of the Beast]], where [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] can be obtained. It is located in the [[Matriarch's Lair]].
## Behavior
The Matriarch generates [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]], generating 3 every 24h. If The Matriarch has 1 or more [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] available, it will attempt to pull the player towards it and eat them. The player will then be transported to the [[Belly of the Beast]].
While in the [[Belly of the Beast]], the player's stats are set to their default values and all effects are disabled. The player will be able to collect Heavy Pearls by hitting the piles of them located around the area 3 times. Each pile grants 1 [[Heavy Pearl]], and the player will be able to collect the amount of [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] that are available. At the same time, stomach acid will rise in the room, dealing heavy damage if the player touches it.
The player can escape the [[Belly of the Beast]] by punching the tonsils, represented by towers of pigs, hanging from the top of the area. If the player dies to The Matriarch, there will be a 10 minute cooldown before they can be eaten again. If all Heavy Pearls have been obtained from The Matriarch, and the cooldown is over, the player can re-enter the [[Belly of the Beast]].
[[Matriarch's Perfume]] can be used to make The Matriarch instantly produce 3 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]]; it can be used up to 4 times per day. Additionally, the [[Attributes|Matriarch Cubs]] attribute grants, at its maximum level, a +20% chance to gain an additional [[Heavy Pearl]] when collecting one, meaning it is possible to get up to 6 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] (the average amount being 3.8 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]]).
## Tips
- You can find the Heavy Pearls' location while being eaten by The Matriarch, to set up the route for getting the Heavy Pearls. You should try to aim for one of the Heavy Pearls, and grab it as soon as the tentacles release you (similar to [[Storm]] boss fight's prefire). Also, when you find the pigs after getting all the pearls needed, spam hitting them to get up before the acid gets up.
- While wearing Unstable Dragon Armor, when trying to escape using the pigs, it has a chance to strike lightning onto all of the pigs at once and lifting you out of The Matriarch without even hitting a pig once.
- While using the [[Flaming Fist]], only one hit is needed to collect a heavy pearl, and only one hit is needed on a tonsil to escape. This may be used as a method to speedrun The Matriarch.
- Unlike [[Time Systems|many other systems in the game which reset daily at 12AM ET]], the cooldown for The Matriarch instead resets daily at 8PM ET.
## Trivia
- The Matriarch contains a Boba Fett helmet within the digestive fluid on the floor, likely referencing his fall into the Great Pit of Carkoon in Star Wars: Return of the Jedi.
- Collecting all 3 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] in the Matriarch without taking damage rewards the [[Achievements|The Perfect Heist]] achievement.
- The word Matriarch usually refers to a woman in charge of a family, a group or a state.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 15, 2022|Added The Matriarch.|
Linked from: [[Achievements]], [[Belly of the Beast]], [[Crimson Isle]], [[Gris]], [[Heavy Pearl]], [[Locations]], [[Locations/Crimson Isle]], [[Magma Chamber]], [[Matriarch's Lair]], [[Matriarch's Perfume]], [[Pearl (disambiguation)]], [[Quests]], [[Spirit Mask]]

The Professor is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Catacombs - Floor III]] of [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Collection
Killing the Professor increases The Professor collection.
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Suspicious Vial]] Reward|+15|
|II|50|Adaptive Leggings ([[Adaptive Armor]]) Reward|+15|
|III|100|Golden Professor Head ([[Golden Heads]]) Reward; Gold ([[Gold Essence]]) Essence|+15|
|IV|150|Adaptive Chestplate ([[Adaptive Armor]]) Reward|+25|
|V|250|[[Recombobulator 3000]] Reward|+25|
|VI|1,000|Diamond Professor Head ([[Diamond Heads]]) Recipe; Diamond ([[Diamond Essence]]) Essence|+25|
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
## Boss Fight
### Phases
The Professor fight has three distinct phases:
|Phase|Description|
|-|-|
|First|In the first phase, the Professor is surrounded by a barrier at the beginning of the fight, preventing players from dealing damage through it. To end the first phase, all of The Professor's Guardians must be defeated. Each guardian has a special attack/ability. The Guardians are revived with 80% (Normal) / 90% (Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])) [[Health]] after 10 seconds (7 in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])) of being killed.|
|Second|In the second phase, all four guardians are dead, and the player must fight the Professor until they reach 25% of their [[Health]], or 750,000 and 150,000,000 in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]).|
|Third|In his third phase, the Professor absorbs the dead guardians and transforms into a Guardian himself. He will spawn Rogue Guardians ([[Rogue Guardian]]), and occasionally use the Giga Heal, TNT Rain, and Focus Laser abilities. If the fight goes on for too long, the arena will flood but spawn in several boats. Once the Professor is defeated, Necron will smite him with lightning and warn the players not to go further before he deals with them himself.|
### Attacks
Below are a list of attacks of each mob. More details about the guardian's attacks can be seen on their respective pages.
#### The Professor
|Attack|Allowed Phases|Description|
|-|-|-|
|Melee|Second|The Professor has a melee attack, dealing damage to players he strikes.|
|Prismarine Blizzard|Second|The Professor summons prismarine blocks in a path towards the player, dealing 5% (15% in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])) of the player's max [[Health]] as [[True Damage]].|
|Laser|Third|The Professor will fire a laser at the player, attacking every 0.75 seconds using its base [[Damage]].|
|TNT Rain|Third|The Professor flings TNT around him in all directions, which detonate and deal its base [[Damage]].|
|Giga Heal|Third|The Professor begins a countdown. At the end of this countdown, he and all the Rogue Guardians ([[Rogue Guardian]]) in the room are healed fully.|
#### [[Chaos Guardian]]
|Ability|Description|
|-|-|
|Discharge|The Chaos Guardian has an Anger Level above its head, which is indicated by the amount of ⚡ lightning icons it has. Hitting the guardian enough times will increase the level, up to level 6. Every two seconds, the Chaos Guardian will strike lightning around itself, damaging players who are nearby. This deals 0.5x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode, or 0.7x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]), multiplied by the Anger Level. Upon reaching Anger Level 6, the level will reset down to 1, and the guardian will prepare to overcharge. Several seconds later, lightning will rapidly strike in the surrounding area for several seconds, dealing the amount of [[Damage]] equivalent to an Anger Level or 12, or 17,280 in normal mode and 453,600 [[Damage]] in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]).|
|Body Slam|Hits itself into the ground when a player gets too close, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (5,760 ), or 6x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) (324,000 ).|
#### [[Healthy Guardian]]
|Ability|Description|
|-|-|
|Giga Heal|The Healthy Guardian will announce that a Giga Heal is about to occur, which will heal all of the alive guardians in 10 seconds (8 in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])). During this time, attacking the Healthy Guardian will reduce the healed amount by 5% per hit. Hitting 20 times will stop all healing from occurring. The base amount healed is 10,000,000 [[Health]], or 210,000,000 [[Health]] in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]). Upon healing, the next announcement will occur 60 seconds later (45s in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])).|
|Body Slam|Hits itself into the ground when a player gets too close, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (8,784 ), or 6x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) (486,000 ).|
#### [[Reinforced Guardian]]
|Ability|Description|
|-|-|
|Higher [[Health]]|The Reinforced Guardian has higher [[Health]] compared to the other guardians.|
|Rogue Guardians ([[Rogue Guardian]])|The Reinforced Guardian will repeatedly spawn Rogue Guardians ([[Rogue Guardian]]) around itself in the air. These will fly around, firing lasers at any nearby players. After being alive for some time, they will turn into Elder Rogue Guardians, which have higher [[Stats]].|
|TNT Rain|Every ~40 seconds (or ~30s in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]])), the Reinforced Guardian will start throwing TNT around itself for 10 seconds. This TNT deals 0.6x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (3,024 ), or 0.8x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) (64,800 ). This timer runs independently of the Reinforged Guardian's alive state, meaning killing it does not impact this timer. If the TNT rain were to start when the boss is otherwise dead, it gets skipped.|
|Body Slam|Hits itself into the ground when a player gets too close, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (10,080 ), or 6x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) (486,000 ).|
#### [[Laser Guardian]]
|Ability|Description|
|-|-|
|Default Laser|By default, the Laser Guardian will fire a standard, slower laser at the player, attacking every 0.75 seconds using its base [[Damage]].|
|Focus Beam|Starting 10s into the fight, the Laser Guardian will begin the following looping process: Announce that the Focus Beam is being charged up. This disables the default laser, and takes 15s in normal mode or 10 seconds in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]). Start firing the higher powered Focus Beam instead of the default laser. This attacks at a faster 0.5s rate and has a higher reach, but only deals 0.1x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (432 ), or 0.6x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) (48,600 ). This laser lasts for 60s in normal mode and 45s in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]). Announce that the Focus Beam has overheated and is on cooldown, which lasts for 10s. Repeat this process. If the Laser Guardian dies, once it respawns, the same cooldown message is sent 5s after, meaning the laser starts building up 15s later.|
|Body Slam|Hits itself into the ground when a player gets too close, dealing immense [[Knockback]], 2x the base [[Damage]] in normal mode (8,640 ), or 6x it in Master Mode ([[Catacombs]]) (486,000 ).|

### Master Mode Changes
- The Professor has 600M [[Health]] and his guardians 120M [[Health]] and 140M [[Health]] . All attacks deal more [[Damage]].
- [[Rogue Guardian|Rogue Guardians]] immediately spawn as the Elder variant.
- Guardians are revived after 7 seconds instead of 10.
- When Guardians are revived, they regain 90% of their [[Health]] instead of 80%.
- The [[Healthy Guardian]] is invulnerable when performing Giga Heal, however, Giga Heal can still be interrupted by hitting the Guardian enough times.

## Trivia
- The Floor III boss was called Oak in the Alpha Hypixel Network.
  - This was a reference to Professor Oak from the Pokémon franchise.
  - It is unknown why it was changed.
- This is the first usage of Elder Guardians in Hypixel SkyBlock.
- This is the first time [[Necron]] interacts with the player.
- [[Necron]] killed The Professor after he was defeated.
- The Professor canonically taught Scarf the Necromancy skills they have; however, he also has other unknown students.
- The Rogue Guardians can be absorbed by Necromancer Sword and Reaper Scythe, making them the only non-undead mob to be able to be summoned.
- His head has the same texture as [[Sadan]]'s, except that it has been recolored and the brows are missing.
- Although the professor is the one who drops [[Adaptive Armor]], the armor is upgraded with [[Scarf Fragment|Scarf Fragments]].

# The Wither King
Aliases: Kaeman, Wither King
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Catacombs, Dungeons
[The Wither King]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: BOSS ([[Bosses]])
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]]
Mob Type: [[Wither]] [[Arcane]]
[Metadata]
ID: MASTER_WITHER_KING_1
The Wither King is the [[Bosses|BOSS]] of [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|The Master Catacombs - Floor VII]].
## Boss Fight
The bottom of Master Mode Floor VII contains a giant throne room. There are five relics placed next to dragon statues around the room. There are also five cauldrons in front of the throne near the center of the room. They each have a specifically colored clay below them. The players must bring and place the relics with the same corresponding color as the cauldron's block of clay, otherwise the players fail the run. After placing the relics, The Wither King will be summoned on his throne.
Damage cannot be dealt towards The Wither King, and he mostly acts as a spectator to the battle. The Wither King will spawn one of five dragons, named [[Apex Dragon]], [[Flame Dragon]], [[Ice Dragon]], [[Power Dragon]] and [[Soul Dragon]], every 30 seconds. All five dragons must be killed near their corresponding colored dragon statue to destroy them. All dragons can respawn at any time indefinitely until all dragon statues are destroyed. If all five statues are destroyed, the floor is completed.
If all five dragons are alive at any time, the Wither King will send the players into the air in the center of a ritual. The dragons will complete the ritual and summon a giant hand coming up through the floor, grabbing and closing around the players, causing them to fail the dungeon run.
### Attacks
- The Wither King will pull the player towards them, instantly killing the player, if the player gets close to the Wither King.
- The Wither King will create a beam that moves around the throne room, dealing [[Damage]] to players on contact.
- The Wither King will strike players with lightning, dealing [[Damage]].

## Lore
- The Wither King's real name is Kaeman, confirmed by himself during his conversation with [[Necron]] in the [[Journal|Journal Entry - The Apprentice 3]].
- Kaeman was a top student at the Mage Academy. After his studies, he became a researcher in the field of advanced magic. One day, a farmhand, Sarah, had been hurt while in the fields, and Kaeman was charged with healing her. They fell in love. They soon started living together and Kaeman's priorities shifted, he devoted less time to research and more to family, while still an active mage. Not long after, they had a child, Mylah.
- Out of nowhere, [[Jythar|Jythar, the Dragon Emperor]] launched an attack on the human realm with his entire dragon army. Kaeman and other mages made their way to defend the castle. Mages laid a trap atop the mountain, which when Jythar landed on, would crush him with the immense force of gravity, and he fell right into it. Other dragons shielded their emperor with their lives, though many of them died. Against all odds, Jythar rose to his feet, he used the [[Fels|Fel Magic]] - the magic of the dead. In a final act of vengeance, he decimated the earth itself using magic so powerful that it created a [[The Breach|hole in space]] on the mountain's peak and [[Rift Dimension|another dimension]]. Jythar laid waste to all in his path, sparing not even his dragons. In the end, the previously connected realm was separated into islands, and Jythar's corpse fell to an area now known as [[Dragontail]].
- As the Emperor fell, Kaeman's mind raced to his loved ones, but unfortunately, he was too late. Grieving, a thought crossed his mind: if Jythar could use magic to cheat death for vengeance, maybe he could use the same magic to revive his family. Jythar's heart, the core of the Fel Magic, was placed on an island to not cause any trouble. However, it corrupted the island and from it came new fauna. Kaeman set up a laboratory on this island, [[The End]].
- He began researching [[Fels|Fel Magic]] to try and bring them back to life. After seven years, he tried the spell for the first time, which reanimated them, but they weren't themselves. After three more years of research, during which Kaeman started venturing further from his laboratory, castle's routine inspection discovered the facility, stole the scrolls, killed Sarah and Mylah and destroyed what was left of the lab. After this loss, Kaeman sought vengeance casting a spell and willing to give his life for it so he could be reunited with his family. That's when he understood the essence of Fel magic, power of sacrifice. After giving up his life, he has gained more power than ever before. This spell also merged him with the remains of his family, with Mylah's head on his left and Sarah's on his right. Fueled by burning anger, he charged towards the castle, a decade after the Calamity, determined to unleash upon them a terror greater than they had ever known. After this destruction, Kaeman was defeated by mages and imprisoned at the edge of the main island.
- Years later, someone stole Kaeman's scrolls from Mages and given it to a young not very successful mage student - [[Necron]]. He has learned this spell and used it to help Kaeman free himself, leading to a disturbance that affected even the [[Rift Dimension]], and created the [[Broken Cage]] area there. Kaeman then took Necron to the Catacombs.
- After Necron brought [[The Watcher|Lathrop]] with him to [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]], Kaeman brought him in front of the the [[The Watcher|Seeing-Stone]], which triggered visions in Lathrop was followed his entire life that were somehow connected to the Breach above the [[Mountain]]. Over the next few months, Lathrop spent several hours a day looking at the Seeing-Stone, increasing his control over his visions and allowing him to see into the Kaeman's past and becoming [[The Watcher]]. The Watcher was then tasked to reshape the Seeing-Stone for another purpose, so he studied the Wither King's past deeper and, using the orb, he created a world identical to the one Kaeman used to know. The Wither King then spent days in this realm and said he has no need for Watcher anymore, charging him to be loyal to Necron who has taken over the Catacombs since.
## Trivia
- The tentacles coming out of The Wither King's body could be based off of the Wither Storm from Minecraft: Story Mode.
- The Piglin [[Sirih]] mentions the Wither King's original life.
  - This is confirmed in the [[Journal|Journal Memories]].
- When The Wither King is summoned, he has an obfuscated name.
- It’s implied by the line “You did what I couldn’t do myself” after death that The Wither King had attempted to take his own life, but failed likely due to his own power not being enough to kill him.

A Thrall will walk towards the player and try to do melee attacks while they are close to the Thrall. If a Thrall hits the player, they will take away 8 [[Rift Time]] and 0.5 [[Hearts]] from the player.

The Thyst is a mob that has a chance to spawn from breaking [[Gemstone|Amethyst]] in [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Thysts have a 20% chance to spawn from breaking [[Gemstone|Amethyst]] in [[Crystal Hollows]]. Thysts can also spawn when [[Gemstone|Amethyst]] is broken using the [[Armadillo Pet]]'s Tunneler Ability.
## Behavior
Thysts will move towards and leap at the player, dealing 250 [[Damage]] on contact. They have 5000 [[Health]] .

# Timestalk Clone
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Timestalk Clone]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 1
Spawn Location: [[The Garden]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 20; [[Defense]]: 10,000; [[Damage]]: 50
[Drops]

XP: +5 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Coins]]: 1 Coin ([[Coins]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 1
[Metadata]
ID: TIMESTALK_CLONE_100
A Timestalk Clone is a [[Mob]] that spawns from harvesting [[Timestalk]] in the [[The Garden]].
## Spawning
Harvesting a [[Timestalk]] creates a Timestalk Clone that uses the skin, armor, and held item of the player that broke the mutation.
## Behavior
A Timestalk Clone walks towards the player to attack.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv1]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Timestalk]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv1]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|1|1|1|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|5|5|5|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|1|1|1|
Timestalk Clone can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Timestalk Clone Shard]] and +1,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+10,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Bestiary
|Section|Family|Lore|Bracket|Max Tier|Max Kills|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|The Garden ([[Bestiary/List]])|Timestalk Clone|There can only be one, sometimes two apparently.|7|10|20|
Rewards are earned after reaching each tier, providing bonus stats and perks against mobs in that family.
|Tier|Kills|[[Coins]]| [[Experience]] orbs|[[Farming Fortune]]* (Garden Bonus: Farming Fortune is given as a base stat applicable everywhere)|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|Ⅰ|1|+2%|+0.5|+0.4 [[Farming Fortune]]|+1 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅱ|2|+4%|+1|+0.8 [[Farming Fortune]]|+2 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅲ|3|+6%|+1.5|+1.2 [[Farming Fortune]]|+3 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅳ|5|+8%|+2|+1.6 [[Farming Fortune]]|+4 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅴ|7|+10%|+2.5|+2 [[Farming Fortune]]|+5 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅵ|9|+12%|+3|+2.4 [[Farming Fortune]]|+6 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅶ|11|+14%|+3.5|+2.8 [[Farming Fortune]]|+7 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅷ|14|+16%|+4|+3.2 [[Farming Fortune]]|+8 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅸ|17|+18%|+4.5|+3.6 [[Farming Fortune]]|+9 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|Ⅹ|20|+20%|+5|+4 [[Farming Fortune]]|+10 XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
## Trivia
- The max [[Bestiary]] level of Timestalk Clone is 10, equaling 20 kills.
  - It, alongside [[Field Mouse]], [[Zombuddy]], and the [[Lunar Moth]], are the only mobs on [[The Garden]] to not require 250 kills.
- Despite being in the Garden [[Bestiary]] category, it is immune to [[Vacuums]] and doesn't grant a [[Pests (Currency)|Pest]] when slain. However, each Bestiary tier still grants 0.4 [[Farming Fortune]].
- Both Timestalk Clones and [[Phantom Spirit|Phantom Spirits]] copy the player's skin; The former upon spawning, while the latter does so upon attacking a player.

## Tips
- Treasure Hoarders are a great source of [[Coins]] for early game. Assuming the player is able to kill all 6 Treasure Hoarders every respawn cycle (10s), it will result in a profit of per cycle or per hour from [[Starfall]] alone. This is then multiplied based on the player's [[Looting]] level.
  - With 200 [[Magic Find]] (including [[Bestiary]]) and [[Looting|Looting IV]], players can expect another from [[Treasurite]] for a total of .
  - It is recommended to put [[Kaleidoscopic Mineral]] in a [[Personal Deletor 4000|Personal Deletor]] as they do not stack. Furthermore, they only bring a negligible profit of assuming 500 [[Magic Find]] and [[Looting|Looting V]].
## Trivia
- Treasure Hoarders used to share a skin with [[Charlie]], an NPC found in the [[Birch Park]].
- Treasure Hoarders share a skin with [[Glacite Walker|Glacite Walkers]] under their armor.
  - [[Glacite Walker|Glacite Walkers]] used to also share [[Charlie]]'s skin.

# Trick or Treater
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Spooky Festival
[Trick or Treater]

[Metadata]

Trick or Treater is a [[Spooky|Spooky Mob]] that spawns during [[Spooky Festival|Spooky Festivals]].
## Spawning
Trick or Treaters can spawn in the [[Graveyard]], [[Wilderness]], [[Spider's Den]] and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] at night during a [[Spooky Festival]]. They have a 3.06% chance to be spawned from a [[Trick or Treat Chest]].
## Behavior
Trick or Treaters will run quickly and try to attack the player dealing 50 [[Damage]]. They have 10 [[Health]], and like all other Spooky Mobs, they take only 1 [[Damage]] per hit, which can be increased with items like the [[Ghoul Buster]] and the [[Bat Pet]].

Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Crimson Isle

[Metadata]
ID: UNSTABLE_MAGMA_40; UNSTABLE_MAGMA_60; UNSTABLE_MAGMA_80
Unstable Magma is a [[Magma Cube]] type [[Mob]] that spawns when fighting [[Magma Boss]].
## Spawning
After each phase of [[Magma Boss]], it hides and spawns Unstable Magmas and [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]].
## Behavior
After being spawned by [[Magma Boss]], Unstable Magmas leap towards the player, dealing damage on contact. If they fall in the lava pit in the middle of the arena, they are launched away from it.
After enough of them are killed, both Unstable Magmas and [[Magma Glare|Magma Glares]] drift towards the center of arena and [[Magma Boss]] is once again spawned.

# Vanquisher
Categories: Mobs, Templates with deprecated argument names, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crimson Isle
[Vanquisher]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 235
Spawn Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Mob Type: [[Wither]] [[Elusive]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 5,000,000; [[Damage]]: 3,000
[Drops]
Drops: 3x [[Nether Star]] 0–1x [[Cyclamen Dye]]
XP: +352 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 258
[Metadata]
ID: VANQUISHER_100
The Vanquisher is a [[Mob]] that spawns rarely in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Spawning
Vanquishers have a base chance of 1/640 to spawn when any enemy is killed in the [[Crimson Isle]]. It gains heavy damage resistance against all player attacks within the first few seconds of spawning.
Multiple factors can increase the chance of a Vanquisher spawning:
- After 640, 960, and 1,280 mobs are killed without spawning a Vanquisher, the chances are increased.
- The [[Kuudra Pet]]'s Wither Bait perk increases the chances for a Vanquisher to spawn by 0.1-20%.
- [[Attributes|Crimson Serendipity]] ( [[Inferno Koi Shard]]) increases the chances for a Vanquisher to spawn by 2-20%.
- Since Vanquishers are [[Elusive]], the chances for a Vanquisher to spawn are affected by [[Tracking]].
|Mechanic|Spawn Rate Bonus|Final Spawn Rate|
|-|-|-|
|640 mobs killed without Vanquisher|×2|1/320|
|960 mobs killed without Vanquisher|×3|1/213.3; or 3/640|
|1,280 mobs killed without Vanquisher|×4|1/160|
|[Lv100] COMMON Kuudra Pet|×1.1|1/576|
|[Lv100] UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) or RARE ([[Rarity]]) Kuudra Pet|×1.15|1/544|
|[Lv100] EPIC ([[Rarity]]) or LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) Kuudra Pet|×1.2|1/512|
|15 [[Tracking]]|×1.15|1/544|
|Wither Piper perk ([[Essence Shops]])|×1.1|1/576|
Combining all of the following:
- [[Essence Shops|Wither Piper perk]]
- [Lv100] [[Rarity|EPIC]] or [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] Kuudra Pet
- [[Attributes|Crimson Serendipity X]]
- Maximum [[Tracking]]
The spawn chance becomes about 1/351.34 or 0.285%.

# Villager (Stranded)
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data
This content is exclusive to the [[Stranded]] gamemode.
[Villager]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 9
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 1,000; [[Damage]]: 24
[Drops]
XP: +13 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 9
[Metadata]
ID: ISLAND_VILLAGER_#
The Villager is a [[Mob]] that can be spawned from curing [[Zombie Villager (Stranded)|Zombie Villagers]]. Players can trade with Villagers for [[Emerald|Emeralds]] and [[Enchanted Emerald|Enchanted Emeralds]].
## Spawning
A Villager is spawned after curing a [[Zombie Villager (Stranded)|Zombie Villager]] by being fed a [[Golden Apple]] while under the [[Weakness Potion|Weakness]] effect.
## Trading
Players can sell some items to Villagers in exchange for [[Emerald|Emeralds]] and [[Enchanted Emerald|Enchanted Emeralds]]. Players can then exchange these Emeralds and Enchanted Emeralds for [[Pets]], [[Dark Auction]] items, and items from [[Shops|Merchant NPCs]], among other things.
Villagers level up after a set number of [[Emerald|Emeralds]] traded. Each time a Villager levels up, it unlocks more trades.
The current trade level is displayed in the Villager trading interface.

# Vittomite
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows
[Vittomite]
Type: [[Mob]]
Category: MINIBOSS ([[Minibosses]])
Mob Level: 73
Spawn Condition: From [[Executive Viper]]
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]]; [[Ender]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 6,500; [[Damage]]: 450
[Drops]
XP: +547 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 219
[Metadata]
ID: VITTOMITE_10
A Vittomite is a [[Mob]] and [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] that spawns as a projectile from [[Executive Viper]], a [[Team Treasurite]] member found in the [[Mithril Deposits]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Vittomites can be spawned when fighting [[Executive Viper]]. After a few seconds, it will despawn, before another one gets spawned again.
## Behavior
Vittomites will crawl towards the player it was shot towards to attack them. It despawns after a few seconds.
